[His team’s current projects](https://github.com/Intelligent-Internet).\n\n1. **Record a 90-second demo.** Give agents a useful task, interrupt a session, resume it with decisions intact, and show the finished result and its verification. Pick something reproducible.\n2. **Connect it to his ownership thesis.** Intelligent Internet emphasizes AI people own and control. Show concretely how Papercusp supports that through a workspace people run themselves. [Current positioning](https://ii.inc/).\n3. **Seek a warm introduction if you have a genuine mutual contact.** Otherwise, use a concise public message or the [official II contact route](https://ii.inc/contact). Include the clip.\n4. **Ask for a 15-minute guided trial.** Let him or an II engineer choose the task. Have your environment ready so the first interaction produces useful work.\n\nOnce the demo exists, send something like:\n\n> Emad, I’ve been following Zenith and CommonGround. I’m building Papercusp, a self-hosted workspace for coordinating agents with persistent decisions and completion evidence. This 90-second demo shows a task surviving an interrupted session and finishing with a checked result: [link]. Interested in trying one real II task together for 15 minutes? I’d value seeing where it breaks.\n\nFollow up once with fresh evidence after about a week. The requester, su-3a3a10c1, has a threaded reply, and I closed their older review request, which was still marked unanswered.\n\nResults per meta criterion:\n\n| Meta criterion | Verdict | Basis |\n|---|---|---|\n| Outcomes, not diff restatements | Pass | All 18 criteria are observable outcomes (benchmark numbers, failure-path behavior, state after an operation, a docs search). Of the uncited Decisions, three are procedural amendment records and four are superseded R-18 methods. D-023 is the only uncited one that should be graded. |\n| Falsifiable with evidence | Pass | Every criterion has numbered \"BROKEN if\" conditions with concrete numbers or failure states. None relies on adjectives alone. |\n| Failure-mode coverage | Pass, one gap | Covers missing preload, the kill-switch, write load, bloat, retirement without a soak, websearch operators, the exact-list cap, and invalid or seed-chased runs. D-023's stale-state guard on `bm25_get_current_score` is not covered. |\n| Non-overlapping | Pass, one note | One recall loss fails both R-13 and R-18.
